KKT condition for interior optimum of min max c_t with sum=1:
There is M and multipliers λ_t≥0, support active {t: c_t=M}, such that for all i with a_i>0:
∑_{t∈A} λ_t ∂c_t/∂a_i = ρ (constant).
∂c_t/∂a_i = 
- 2 a_j if i+j=t, i≠j
- a_i if i=t-i (i=t/2)

2. Compute c.

Implement crosscorr_gradient(p,y):
```
def gradient_from_weights(p, y):
    n=len(y); L=2*n-1
    fsize=1<<((L+n-1).bit_length()) # 3n-2 -> pow
    # p_rev = p[::-1]
    P = fft(p[::-1], fsize)
    Y = fft(y, fsize)
    G = np.real(ifft(P * Y))  # conv(y,p_rev)
    # G index L-1-k for k=0..n-1
    start = L-1-(n-1) = L-n = n-1? Since L=2n-1, L-n = n-1.
    # G[n-1 : 2n-1?] Let's check: k=0 index L-1 =2n-2; k=n-1 index L-1-(n-1)=L-n=n-1. So from n-1 to 2n-2 length n, reversed? We can take G[n-1:2n-1][::-1]? Wait mapping k -> index L-1-k. If we slice G[n-1:2n-1], indices n-1, n, ...,2n-2 correspond k=n-1,...,0. So G_slice[::-1] gives k=0..n-1.
    g = 2*G_slice[::-1]
    # diag correction: for k where 2k<L: g[k] -= p[2k]*y[k]
    valid=2*np.arange(n)<L
    g[valid] -= p[2*valid]*y[valid]
    return g
```

def make_p(C, mode, q, beta, threshold_frac):
    maxC=C.max(); meanC=C.mean()
    if mode == 'lse':
        gap=max(1e-12, maxC - median or mean)
        beta = min(beta_max, beta0 / gap? Actually beta units 1/C difference. Use beta = target_score / gap.
        s=beta*(C-maxC); p=exp(s); p/=sum
    elif 'topq':
        thr = meanC + frac*(maxC-meanC)
        diff=np.maximum(C-thr,0); w=diff**q; if sum=0 fallback top1
        p=w/sum
```

At current y, identify active peak t (max C_t). To reduce C_t, need reduce products y_i y_j for i+j=t. We can move small mass from a coordinate with high contribution to a low-contribution coordinate. For each i, contribution to peak t is y_{t-i}. If we move δ from i to j:
Δ C_t ≈ 2δ( y_{t-j} - y_{t-i}) + ... For same t. We want choose i high y_{t-i}, j low y_{t-j}. Also consider other peaks. This is like gradient g: move according to g_i = ∑_{active t} y_{t-i}. Move from high g to low g. Projected gradient does exactly overall.

Can we compute `normalize_log(u)`:
```
def normalize_from_log(u):
    umax = u.max()
    w = np.exp(u - umax)
    s = w.sum()
    if s==0: fallback uniform
    return w * (n/s)
```

Let's design `mirror_optimize(y0, budget, seed, score_start=3, score_end=30, eta_scale=0.2, restarts...)`:
Pseudo:
```
def mirror_optimize(y0, budget, rng, score_start=3.0, score_end=30.0, eta0=0.3, patience=400, verbose=False):
   n=len(y0); deadline=time+ budget
   y = project_simplex(y0, n)  # ensure
   logy = np.log(np.maximum(y, LOG_FLOOR))
   C = conv(y)
   bestC=C.max(); best_y=y.copy(); best_obj=2*bestC/n
   it=0; stuck=0; patience_cur=patience; prev_best=bestC
   while time<deadline:
      progress = min(1, (time-start)/budget)
      score = score_start * (score_end/score_start)**progress
      C = conv(y)  # maybe reuse? after update need C; ensure fresh
      maxC=C.max()
      if maxC < bestC - 1e-9: update best, stuck=0 else stuck++
      gap = maxC - np.median(C)
      if gap < 1e-8: break
      beta = min(EGA?, score/gap)
      # compute p stable
      s = beta*(C - maxC)
      # if beta*maxC > 300 (s min < -300), scale? Actually s min = -beta*maxC ~ -score*max/gap. If score=30, max/gap=1.5, min=-45 okay. fine.
      p=exp(s); psum; if psum==0: p onehot max
      # gradient
      g = grad_from_weights(p,y)
      # optional symmetrize? no.
      # scale eta
      gmax = np.max(g)
      eta = eta0 / max(gmax, 1e-12)  # typical 0.3
      # line search over alpha factors [1,0.5,0.25,2?] maybe
      best_alpha=None; best_candidate_obj=maxC
      for alpha in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 2.0):
          u = logy - alpha*eta*g
          y_c = normalize_log(u,n)
          C_c=conv(y_c); objC=C_c.max()
          if objC < best_candidate_obj: best_candidate_obj=objC; best_y_c=y_c; best_alpha=alpha
      if best_y_c is None:
          # no improvement, maybe accept smaller step 0.1 or perturbation
          y = normalize_log(logy - 0.1*eta*g, n)
      else:
          y=best_y_c; logy=np.log(max(y, floor))
      if stuck>patience:
          # noise in log
          noise = rng.normal(0, 0.1, n) * (1-progress?)
          logy = logy + noise
          y=normalize_log(logy,n)
          stuck=0; patience*=1.2
   return best_y, best_obj
```

L=2*n-1
size=1 << (L-1).bit_length()
F = rfft(y, size)
C = irfft(F*F, size)
C = C[:L]
C[C<0]=0? small negative; leave.
```
Gradient:
```
def grad_weights(p,y):
   n=len(y); L=2*n-1
   gsize=1 << (L + n - 2).bit_length() # length up to 3n-2, bit_length
   P = rfft(p[::-1], gsize)
   Y = rfft(y, gsize)
   G = irfft(P*Y, gsize)
   start = n-1
   Gslice = G[start:start+n][::-1]
   g = 2*Gslice
   idx = np.arange(n)
   valid = 2*idx < L
   g[valid] -= p[2*idx[valid]] * y[valid]
   return g
```
Need ensure p maybe length L. p[::-1] copies. Could optimize by `p[::-1]` okay.

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft

LOG_FLOOR = 1e-30

def fft_convolve(self): ...
def objective_from_conv(C,n): return 2*C.max()/n
def project_simplex(v,S): ...
def normalize_log(u,n): ...
def weights_lse(C,maxC,score): ...
def weights_soft(C,maxC,score,q): ...
def weights_topk(C,k): ...
def gradient_from_weights(p,y): ...

Implementation details for mirror_optimize:
Arguments: y0, time_budget, rng, score_start=6, score_end=30, eta_scale=0.4, weight_mode='lse', q=2, patience=300, max_iters? Use while time.
Return best_y, best_obj.
Pseudo code carefully:
```
def mirror_optimize(y0, budget_s, rng, score_start=6.0, score_end=35.0, eta_base=0.4, weight_mode='lse', q=2.0, patience_start=300, verbose=False):
    start=time.monotonic()
    deadline=start+budget_s
    n=len(y0)
    y = project_simplex(y0, float(n))
    C = fft_convolve(y)
    bestC = C.max(); best_y=y.copy(); best_obj=2*bestC/n
    logy = np.log(np.maximum(y, LOG_FLOOR))
    it=0; stuck=0; patience=patience_start
    prev_alpha=1.0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it+=1
        maxC = C.max()
        if maxC < bestC - 1e-12:
            bestC=maxC; best_y=y.copy(); stuck=0
        else:
            stuck+=1
        if stuck > patience:
            # perturb logy with noise, recompute
            noise_amp = 0.05 + 0.1*(stuck/patience)
            logy = logy + rng.normal(0, noise_amp/np.sqrt(n)?, n)  # amplitude?
            y = normalize_log(logy,n)
            C = fft_convolve(y)
            stuck=0; patience=int(patience*1.15)
            continue
        progress = min(1.0, (time.monotonic()-start)/budget_s)
        score = score_start * (score_end/score_start)**progress
        medianC = np.median(C)
        gap = maxC - medianC
        if gap <= 1e-12: break
        if weight_mode == 'lse':
             beta = score / gap
             # if beta*maxC too high? If maxC/gap large, beta*maxC ~score*max/gap. Could be >700 if maxC/gap ~700/score? maxC/gap often <10. fine.
             s = beta*(C - maxC)
             # clip min to -745 to avoid underflow warnings
             s = np.maximum(s, -745.0)
             p = np.exp(s)
             psum = p.sum()
             if psum <= 0 or not finite: p oneargmax
             p /= psum
        elif 'soft':
             threshold = maxC - gap/min(score, 1e-6)
             diff = np.maximum(C - threshold, 0)
             w = diff**q
             sumw = w.sum(); if sumw==0 fallback top1
             p=w/sumw
        elif 'topk':
             k = int(max(1, 5 + 100*progress)) # maybe
             top = np.argpartition(C, -k)[-k:]
             p=np.zeros(len(C)); base=C[top].min(); w=C[top]-base; if w.sum()==0: w=np.ones(k)/k; else w/=w.sum(); p[top]=w; p/=p.sum()
        g = gradient_from_weights(p,y)
        # compute h scale
        finite = np.isfinite(g)
        if not all or g max 0: break
        gmax = np.quantile(g, 0.99) # or max
        if gmax <= 1e-14: break
        eta = eta_base / gmax
        # alphas include previous
        alphas = [prev_alpha, 0.5*prev_alpha, 2.0*prev_alpha, 0.25*prev_alpha]
        unique >0
        best_alpha=prev_alpha; best_candidate=None; best_cand_max = maxC
        # Add small alpha 1e-3 maybe
        for a in alphas:
            if a<=0: continue
            u = logy - a*eta*g
            y_c = normalize_log(u,n)
            C_c = fft_convolve(y_c)
            mc = C_c.max()
            if mc < best_cand_max - 1e-12:
                best_cand_max=mc; best_candidate=(y_c,C_c); best_alpha=a
        if best_candidate is None:
            # try smaller step fixed 0.05*eta? or accept if very close? Use current direction with tiny step to avoid stuck?
            # If no decrease, increase score? We can just perturb if stuck soon. To keep moving, accept candidate with least max among alphas even if not improved (if not too worse?) maybe no.
            # Use current gradient with alpha=0.05 to see? Already tried. If none, do a small mirror step with alpha=0.02 (if finite) and continue; objective may increase but perhaps long-term.
            a_small = min(alphas)/10.0 if alphas else 0.01
            u=logy - a_small*eta*g
            y=normalize_log(u,n); C=fft_convolve(y)
        else:
            y,C=best_candidate; logy=np.log(np.maximum(y,LOG_FLOOR)); prev_alpha=best_alpha
        # occasional print every 2000 iters
    return best_y, 2*bestC/n
```

def projected_optimize(y0,budget,rng, score_start=10,end=60, eta=0.5):
 y=project_simplex(y0,n)
 C=conv; best...
 v=zeros; momentum=0.85
 while time:
   maxC=C.max(); update best/stuck
   score ... p=LSE
   g=grad(p,y)
   gnorm=||g||; if small break
   alpha0 = 2*max(1e-12, maxC-bestC)/(gnorm*gnorm) + maybe 1/gnorm*0.1
   v = momentum*v + (1-momentum)*alpha0*g

      y_c=project_simplex(y - fac*v, n)
      C_c=conv; mc=...
      accept best/few
   if none/noise
```

```
def make_weights_lse(C, maxC, meanC, score):
    gap = maxC-meanC
    if gap <= 1e-30: p uniform? return onehot max.
    beta = score / gap
    s = beta*(C-maxC)
    s = np.clip(s, -700, 0)
    p=np.exp(s)
    ssum=p.sum()
    if ssum<=0: p[argmax]=1
    else p/=ssum
    return p
```

w=diff**q
sumw; p=w/sumw
```

Now, upsample function:
```
def upsample_y(y, n_target):
    n=len(y)
    if n_target == n: return y.copy()
    t_old=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
    t_new=np.linspace(0,1,n_target,endpoint=False)
    v=np.interp(t_new,t_old,y)
    v=np.maximum(v,0)
    return project_simplex(v, float(n_target))
```
Linear interpolation may create smooth. For n target not multiple, okay. Could use spectral resampling to preserve asymptotic. Maybe implement `upsample_spectral`: use rfft of y, zero pad to size n_target? But nonperiodic; not straightforward. Linear okay.

Could also use `np.interp` with periodic extension? Sequence not periodic; endpoint at 1 corresponds y[0]? If we treat periodic, linear wrap. But shape at endpoints important; periodic interpolation may blend edge to start, maybe distort. Use non-periodic endpoint=False. The last interval from n-1 to n (value y0?) not included. Fine.

If y has sharp first value high, linear to zero at x=1? Since endpoint=False, intervals [0,1/n),..., [ (n-1)/n,1) from y_{n-1} to y_0? np.interp only rises? It doesn't know after last; values outside use endpoints? For t_new max (1-1/m) less than 1, okay. It connects t_old last to? It linearly between last two for last intervals? No, for t in last interval between t_old[-2] and t_old[-1], okay. No wrap. It doesn't connect to start, so boundary at x=1 is just y[-1] extended? For t_new beyond last old? no. Thus endpoint 0 to1 not periodic. Good.

Pseudo code exact:
```
def mirror_optimize(y0, budget_s, rng, mode='lse', score_start=6.0, score_end=40.0, eta_start=0.35, eta_end=1.2, momentum=0.85, patience_start=300, q=2.0, topk_start=5, topk_end=120, verbose=False):
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+budget_s
    n=len(y0)
    y=project_simplex(y0, float(n))
    C=fft_convolve(y)
    bestC=C.max(); best_y=y.copy(); best_obj=2*bestC/float(n)
    logy=np.log(np.maximum(y, LOG_FLOOR))
    d=np.zeros(n)
    it=0; stuck=0; patience=patience_start; fail=0; prev_alpha=1.0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        maxC = float(np.max(C))
        if maxC < bestC - 1e-12:
             bestC=maxC; best_y=y.copy(); best_obj=2*bestC/n; stuck=0
        else:
             stuck += 1
        if stuck > patience:
             # noise
             amp=0.08 + 0.05*min(1.0, stuck/(patience*2))
             logy = logy + rng.normal(0.0, amp, n)
             y=normalize_log(logy,n)
             C=fft_convolve(y)
             d.fill(0.0)
             stuck=0; fail=0; patience=int(patience*1.15)+50
             if verbose...
             continue
        progress = min(1.0, max(0.0, (time.monotonic()-start)/budget_s))
        score = score_start * (score_end/score_start)**progress
        meanC = (float(y.sum())**2) / (2*n-1) # should n^2/(2n-1)
        gap = maxC - meanC
        if gap <= 1e-12: break
        C_for_weights = C
        if mode == 'lse': p=make_weights_lse(...)
        elif ...
        else p=make_weights_lse(...)
        g=gradient_from_weights(p,y)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)): continue
        g = g - np.mean(g)
        d = momentum*d + (1-momentum)*g
        dmax = np.max(np.abs(d))
        if dmax < 1e-12:
             # no direction; noise
             patience=50; continue? 
        d = d / dmax
        eta = eta_start + (eta_end-eta_start)*progress
        alphas = [1.0, 0.5, 2.0, 0.25]
        # ensure prev included? Use [prev_alpha, 0.5*prev_alpha, 2.0*prev_alpha, 0.25]
        alphas = [prev_alpha, max(0.05, 0.5*prev_alpha), max(0.05, 2.0*prev_alpha), 0.25]
        # unique with tolerance
        best_alpha=prev_alpha; best_y_c=None; best_C_c=None; best_mc=maxC
        min_alpha=None; min_y_c=None; min_C_c=None; min_mc=maxC*1.02 + 1e-8? for acceptable min
        for a in alphas:
             if a<=0: continue
             u=logy - a*eta*d
             y_c=normalize_log(u,n)
             C_c=fft_convolve(y_c)
             mc=float(np.max(C_c))
             if mc < min_mc_abs? track
             if mc < min_mc:
                 min_mc=mc; min_y_c=y_c; min_C_c=C_c; min_alpha=a
             if mc < best_mc - 1e-12:
                 best_mc=mc; best_y_c=y_c; best_C_c=C_c; best_alpha=a
        accepted=False
        if best_y_c is not None:
             y=best_y_c; C=best_C_c; logy=np.log(...); prev_alpha=best_alpha; accepted=True
        elif min_y_c is not None and min_mc < maxC*1.01 + 1e-9 and stuck > patience//4:
             # accept best candidate even if not strictly better
             y=min_y_c; C=min_C_c; logy=...; prev_alpha=min_alpha; accepted=True
             # don't reset fully; stuck=int(stuck*0.5)
             stuck=int(stuck*0.5)
        if not accepted:
             fail +=1
             if fail > 60:
                 # small perturbation in d? or score boost by temporary mode? 
                 # Add small random to logy and reset momentum
                 logy = logy + rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, n)
                 y=normalize_log(logy,n)
                 C=fft_convolve(y)
                 d.fill(0.0)
                 fail=0
        if verbose and it%2000==0: print(...)
    return best_y, best_obj
```

```
def analytic_seed(n, gamma=1.5, phase=0.3, rng=None):
    x=np.arange(n, dtype=float)/n
    y=np.exp(-gamma*x)
    if rng is not None:
       y *= 1.0 + 0.1*np.sin(2*np.pi*x + phase)
       y=np.maximum(y,0)
    return project_simplex(y,n)
```

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0,budget_s-60)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    # load base
    seq = None
    try:
       s = height_sequence_1
       if s is not None:
          seq = np.asarray(s, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    except Exception: seq=None
    if seq is None or len(seq)<128 or not all finite or sum<=0:
        nbase=8192
        seq=analytic_seed(nbase,1.5,0.4,rng)
    else:
        seq=np.clip(seq,0,None)
        nbase=len(seq)
    y = seq / seq.sum() * nbase
    if y[0] < y[-1]: y=y[::-1].copy()
    # maybe normalize with project to ensure
    y = project_simplex(y,float(nbase))
    best_y=y; best_obj=obj_y(y)
    print initial
    # Phase function to optimize with time budget if remaining
    def cannot define nested? Rule no closures. We can directly call multiple times.
    t1 = min(500.0, (deadline-time)*0.25)
    if t1 > 30:
       y1,obj1=mirror_optimize(best_y,t1,rng,'lse',8,40,...)
       if obj1<best_obj: best_y,y=obj1
    t2 = min(300.0, (deadline-time)*0.15)
    if t2 > 30:
       y_start = project_simplex(best_y + rng.normal(0,0.05*max(1,nbase/np.sqrt(nbase)?) actually y scale1, use 0.05, nbase), float(nbase))
       y2,obj2=mirror_optimize(y_start,t2,rng,'soft',4,30,eta_start=0.5,eta_end=1.5)
       update
    # Upsample to 2n
    rem = deadline - time
    n2 = min(32768, 2*nbase) if nbase<32768 else nbase
    if n2>nbase and rem>300:
       t3 = min(900.0, (rem)*0.55) # maybe
       y_up=upsample_y(best_y,n2)
       # orient decreasing
       if y_up[0]<y_up[-1]: reverse
       y3,obj3=mirror_optimize(y_up,t3,rng,'lse',6,35,eta_start=0.25,eta_end=0.9,momentum=0.8)
       update
    # Upsample to 4n1? If rem>200 and best length <32768 and 4*nbase <=32768? Actually after n2, maybe n4=4*nbase if best length nbase? Use current best length.
    n3 = min(65536? maybe 32768 enough, 2*len(best_y))

          update
    # Final polish at best length

    if rem>60:

       y5,obj5=mirror_optimize(best_y,t5,rng,'lse',10,50,eta_start=0.1,eta_end=0.5,momentum=0.7,patience=200)
       update
    final = best_y / best_y.sum()
    final = np.clip(final,0,1000)
    if final.sum()<0.01: fallback
    eval=evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in final])
    print
    return [float(x) for x in final]

Functions:
1. `fft_convolve(y)`:
```
def fft_convolve(y):
    n = len(y)
    L = 2*n - 1
    size = 1 << (L - 1).bit_length()
    F = rfft(y, size)
    C = irfft(F * F, size)
    C = C[:L]
    C = np.maximum(C, 0.0)
    return C
```
For n=0? Not called. For n=1, L=1, size=1<<0=1, rfft ok.

2. `objective_from_conv(C,n)`: return 2*maxC/n.

3. `project_simplex(v,S)`: as above. Need handle v length 1. Use:
```
def project_simplex(v, S):
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n=v.size
    if n==0 or S<=0: return np.zeros(n)
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cum=np.cumsum(u)
    idx=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=np.float64)
    theta_vals=(cum - float(S))/idx
    valid = theta_vals <= u
    if np.any(valid):
       rho=int(np.max(np.where(valid)[0]))+1
    else:
       rho=n
    theta=(cum[rho-1]-float(S))/rho
    out=np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    # Due to FP sum adjust
    s=out.sum()
    if s>0:
       out *= float(S)/s
    else:
       out.fill(float(S)/n)
    return out
```
Check if S>sum(v), theta negative, valid? Example v zeros S=n: theta_vals=-n/i <=0 true, rho=n, theta=-1, out=1, sum n, ok.

4. `normalize_log(u,n)`:
```
def normalize_log(u,n):
    u=np.asarray(u,dtype=np.float64)
    u=np.where(np.isfinite(u), u, -700.0)
    umax=u.max()
    if not np.isfinite(umax): return np.full(n, n/float(n? no sum n => 1.0))
    w=np.exp(u-umax)
    s=w.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0:
       return np.ones(n,dtype=float)
    return w * (float(n)/s)
```
If sum should n, uniform is ones. Good.

def make_weights_lse(C, meanC, score):
   maxC=float(C.max())
   gap=maxC-meanC
   L=len(C)
   if gap <= 1e-12:
      p=np.zeros(L); p[int(np.argmax(C))]=1.0; return p
   beta=score/gap
   # If beta*maxC too huge? s min clip. beta could inf if gap tiny. cap beta=1e6? maxC~1e4, gap tiny -> beta huge; s clip; p onehot. Use if beta > 1e12: onehot.
   if beta > 1e12 or not np.isfinite(beta): p onehot
   s=beta*(C-maxC)
   s=np.clip(s, -745.0, 0.0)
   p=np.exp(s)
   sst=p.sum()
   if sst<=0 or not finite: onehot
   p/=sst
   return p
```
If C has negative small, s maybe >0? C-maxC ≤0, okay. `score` positive.

8. `gradient_from_weights(p,y)`:
```
def gradient_from_weights(p,y):
   n=len(y); L=2*n-1
   gsize = 1 << (L + n - 2).bit_length()
   P = rfft(p[::-1], gsize)
   Y = rfft(y, gsize)
   G = irfft(P * Y, gsize)
   start = n - 1
   GS = G[start:start+n]
   g = 2.0 * GS[::-1]
   idx = np.arange(n)
   valid = 2*idx < L
   if np.any(valid):
       g[valid] -= p[2*idx[valid]] * y[valid]
   return g
```
For n=1, L+n-2=1, bit_length 1? (1).bit_length=1, size=2? Wait (L+n-2)=1, 1<<1=2, but conv length 1 can size1? size2 okay, fine. start=0, GS=G[0:1], reverse. valid 0<1 true. Good.

Need ensure p length L. If p maybe has tiny sum not1? We normalize.

def objective_y(y):

    if s<=0: return np.inf
    y2 = (y/s)*float(n)
    C=fft_convolve(y2)

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2.

The code scales the sequence to a density y with sum(y)=n and uses
    E(y) = 2*max(conv(y,y)) / n
which is exactly the evaluation objective.

The main optimizer is entropy/mirror descent on the log-variables.
Active convolution lags are weighted by an adaptive LSE / threshold /
top-k rule, and the resulting gradient is used in a line-searched
exponential update.  Multi-resolution search is used:
  - polish the provided construction,
  - try a perturbed alternate at the same length,
  - upsample to 2x,
  - upsample toward 32768,
  - final polish.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft

LOG_FLOOR = 1e-30


def next_power_of_two(m: int) -> int:
    """Smallest power of two >= m, for m >= 1."""
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def fft_convolve(y: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """Full linear convolution y*y via real FFT."""
    n = len(y)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    size = next_power_of_two(L)
    F = rfft(y, size)
    C = irfft(F * F, size)[:L]
    # Remove tiny negative FFT roundoff.
    return np.maximum(C, 0.0)


def objective_y(y: np.ndarray) -> float:
    """Objective for density y with sum approx len(y)."""
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.clip(y, 0.0, None)
    s = float(np.sum(y))
    if s <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    n = len(y)
    y = (y / s) * float(n)
    C = fft_convolve(y)
    return float(2.0 * np.max(C) / n)


def project_simplex(v: np.ndarray, S: float) -> np.ndarray:
    """Project onto {x >= 0, sum(x) = S}."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = v.size
    if n == 0 or S <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cum = np.cumsum(u)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    theta_vals = (cum - float(S)) / idx
    valid = theta_vals <= u

    if np.any(valid):
        rho = int(np.max(np.where(valid)[0])) + 1
    else:
        rho = n

    theta = (cum[rho - 1] - float(S)) / float(rho)
    out = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)

    ss = float(np.sum(out))
    if ss > 0.0 and np.isfinite(ss):
        out *= float(S) / ss
    else:
        out.fill(float(S) / n)
    return out


def normalize_log(u: np.ndarray, n: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """Normalize exp(u) to sum n, staying in float range."""
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64)
    u = np.where(np.isfinite(u), u, -700.0)
    umax = float(np.max(u))
    if not np.isfinite(umax):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    w = np.exp(u - umax)
    ss = float(np.sum(w))
    if not np.isfinite(ss) or ss <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return w * (float(n) / ss)


def make_weights_lse(C: np.ndarray, meanC: float, score: float) -> np.ndarray:
    """Softmax weights concentrated on large convolution coefficients."""
    L = len(C)
    maxC = float(np.max(C))
    gap = maxC - meanC

    if gap <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(gap):
        p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(C))] = 1.0
        return p

    beta = score / gap
    if not np.isfinite(beta) or beta > 1e12:
        p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(C))] = 1.0
        return p

    s = beta * (C - maxC)
    s = np.clip(s, -745.0, 0.0)
    p = np.exp(s)
    ss = float(np.sum(p))
    if not np.isfinite(ss) or ss <= 0.0:
        p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(C))] = 1.0
        return p
    return p / ss


def make_weights_soft(C: np.ndarray, meanC: float, score: float, q: float) -> np.ndarray:
    """Polynomial threshold weights on coefficients above a high threshold."""
    L = len(C)
    maxC = float(np.max(C))
    gap = maxC - meanC

    if gap <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(gap):
        p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(C))] = 1.0
        return p

    score = max(1.0, float(score))
    threshold = maxC - gap / score
    diff = np.maximum(C - threshold, 0.0)
    ss = float(np.sum(diff))
    if not np.isfinite(ss) or ss <= 0.0:
        p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(C))] = 1.0
        return p

    w = diff ** max(0.5, float(q))
    ss = float(np.sum(w))
    if not np.isfinite(ss) or ss <= 0.0:
        p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(C))] = 1.0
        return p
    return w / ss


def make_weights_topk(C: np.ndarray, k: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """Weights on the current k largest convolution coefficients."""
    L = len(C)
    k = int(min(max(1, k), L))

    if k >= L:
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64)

    if k == 1:
        p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(C))] = 1.0
        return p

    if k < max(2, L // 2):
        idx = np.argpartition(C, -k)[-k:]
    else:
        idx = np.argsort(C)[-k:]

    p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    vals = C[idx]
    base = float(np.min(vals))
    w = vals - base
    ss = float(np.sum(w))
    if not np.isfinite(ss) or ss <= 0.0:
        w = np.full(k, 1.0 / k, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        w = w / ss
    p[idx] = w
    ss = float(np.sum(p))
    if ss > 0.0:
        p /= ss
    else:
        p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(C))] = 1.0
    return p


def gradient_from_weights(p: np.ndarray, y: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Gradient of sum_t p_t * (y*y)_t with respect to y.
    p has length 2*n-1 and is already normalized.
    """
    n = len(y)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    conv_len = L + n - 1
    size = next_power_of_two(conv_len)

    P = rfft(p[::-1], size)
    Y = rfft(y, size)
    G = irfft(P * Y, size)

    start = n - 1
    Gslice = G[start:start + n]
    g = 2.0 * Gslice[::-1]

    # Correct the index i where 2*i == t: double count in 2*G.
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
    valid = 2 * idx < L
    if np.any(valid):
        g[valid] -= p[2 * idx[valid]] * y[valid]

    return g


def orient_decreasing(y: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """Use the reversal symmetry to keep the heavier side at index 0."""
    n = len(y)
    if n < 3:
        return y.copy()
    half = n // 2
    if float(np.sum(y[half:])) > 1.05 * float(np.sum(y[:half])):
        return y[::-1].copy()
    return y.copy()


def analytic_seed(n: int, gamma: float = 1.5, phase: float = 0.4,
                  rng=None) -> np.ndarray:
    """A skewed, asymmetric seed. Reversal symmetry is already broken."""
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    y = np.exp(-gamma * x)
    if phase is not None:
        y *= 1.0 + 0.15 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x + phase)
        y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
    if rng is not None:
        y += rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, n)
        y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
    if float(np.sum(y)) <= 0.0:
        y = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return project_simplex(y, float(n))


def upsample_y(y: np.ndarray, n_target: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """Linearly interpolates a density to a longer grid."""
    n = len(y)
    if n_target == n:
        return project_simplex(y, float(n))
    if n_target <= 1:
        return project_simplex(y, max(1, n_target))

    t_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    t_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_target, endpoint=False)
    v = np.interp(t_new, t_old, y)
    v = np.maximum(v, 0.0)
    return project_simplex(v, float(n_target))


def mirror_optimize(y0, budget_s, rng, mode="lse", score_start=8.0,
                    score_end=35.0, eta_start=0.35, eta_end=1.0,
                    momentum=0.85, patience_start=300, q=2.0,
                    topk_start=5, topk_end=140, verbose=False):
    """
    Entropic / mirror-descent search normalized to sum(y)=n.
    Returns (best_y, best_objective).
    """
    n = len(y0)
    if budget_s <= 0.0:
        y = project_simplex(y0, float(n))
        return y.clone() if hasattr(y, "clone") else y.copy(), objective_y(y)

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + float(budget_s)

    y = project_simplex(y0, float(n))
    y = orient_decreasing(y)

    C = fft_convolve(y)
    bestC = float(np.max(C))
    best_y = y.copy()
    best_obj = 2.0 * bestC / float(n)

    logy = np.log(np.maximum(y, LOG_FLOOR))
    d = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    it = 0
    stuck = 0
    patience = int(patience_start)
    fail = 0
    prev_alpha = 1.0

    meanC = (float(n) * float(n)) / float(2 * n - 1)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        maxC = float(np.max(C))

        if maxC < bestC - 1e-12:
            bestC = maxC
            best_y = y.copy()
            best_obj = 2.0 * bestC / float(n)
            stuck = 0
        else:
            stuck += 1

        # Random log-perturbation when a stagnation trigger fires.
        if stuck > patience:
            amp = 0.12 + 0.10 * min(1.0, (stuck - patience) / max(1, patience))
            logy = logy + rng.normal(0.0, amp, n)
            y = normalize_log(logy, n)
            C = fft_convolve(y)
            d.fill(0.0)
            stuck = 0
            fail = 0
            patience = int(patience * 1.15) + 50
            continue

        elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
        progress = min(1.0, max(0.0, elapsed / float(budget_s)))
        score = score_start * (score_end / score_start) ** progress

        gap = maxC - meanC
        if gap <= 1e-10 or not np.isfinite(gap):
            break

        if mode == "lse":
            p = make_weights_lse(C, meanC, score)
        elif mode == "soft":
            p = make_weights_soft(C, meanC, score, q)
        elif mode == "topk":
            k = int(topk_start + (topk_end - topk_start) * progress)
            p = make_weights_topk(C, k)
        else:
            p = make_weights_lse(C, meanC, score)

        g = gradient_from_weights(p, y)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            continue

        # In mirror descent, constants in the gradient are irrelevant.
        g = g - float(np.mean(g))
        d = momentum * d + (1.0 - momentum) * g

        dmax = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
        if not np.isfinite(dmax) or dmax < 1e-11:
            stuck += max(1, patience // 8)
            d.fill(0.0)
            continue

        d = d / dmax
        eta = eta_start + (eta_end - eta_start) * progress

        raw_alphas = [
            prev_alpha,
            max(0.08, 0.5 * prev_alpha),
            max(0.15, 1.8 * prev_alpha),
            0.22,
        ]
        alphas = []
        for a in raw_alphas:
            if a > 0.0 and 0.05 <= a <= 2.5:
                if not alphas or abs(a - alphas[-1]) > 1e-3:
                    alphas.append(float(a))
        if not alphas:
            alphas = [0.5]

        best_cand = None
        best_cand_C = None
        best_cand_alpha = prev_alpha
        best_cand_max = maxC

        min_cand = None
        min_cand_C = None
        min_cand_alpha = prev_alpha
        min_cand_max = float("inf")

        for a in alphas:
            u = logy - a * eta * d
            y_c = normalize_log(u, n)
            C_c = fft_convolve(y_c)
            mc = float(np.max(C_c))

            if mc < min_cand_max:
                min_cand = y_c
                min_cand_C = C_c
                min_cand_alpha = a
                min_cand_max = mc

            if mc < best_cand_max - 1e-12:
                best_cand = y_c
                best_cand_C = C_c
                best_cand_alpha = a
                best_cand_max = mc

        accepted = False
        if best_cand is not None:
            y = best_cand
            C = best_cand_C
            logy = np.log(np.maximum(y, LOG_FLOOR))
            prev_alpha = best_cand_alpha
            fail = 0
            accepted = True
        elif min_cand is not None and min_cand_max < maxC * 1.01 + 1e-9 \
                and stuck > patience // 4:
            y = min_cand
            C = min_cand_C
            logy = np.log(np.maximum(y, LOG_FLOOR))
            prev_alpha = min_cand_alpha
            stuck = max(0, int(stuck * 0.5))
            fail = 0
            accepted = True

        if not accepted:
            fail += 1
            if fail > 80:
                # Small local jitter to leave an extremely flat basin.
                logy = logy + rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, n)
                y = normalize_log(logy, n)
                C = fft_convolve(y)
                d.fill(0.0)
                fail = 0

        # Re-use the reversal symmetry to avoid mirror-image wandering.
        if it % 500 == 0 and n > 3:
            half = n // 2
            if float(np.sum(y[half:])) > 1.05 * float(np.sum(y[:half])):
                y = np.ascontiguousarray(y[::-1])
                C = np.ascontiguousarray(C[::-1])
                logy = np.ascontiguousarray(logy[::-1])
                d = np.ascontiguousarray(d[::-1])

        if verbose and it % 5000 == 0:
            print(
                f"    mirror {mode}: it={it}, t={time.monotonic()-start:.0f}s, "
                f"obj={2.0*float(np.max(C))/n:.7f}, best={best_obj:.7f}"
            )

    return best_y.copy(), float(best_obj)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    """
    Main entry point.  Runs for at most budget_s seconds and returns
    a list of non-negative heights minimizing the evaluation objective.
    """
    total_start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = total_start + max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 60.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    # Load the best known construction if available.
    seq = None
    try:
        raw = height_sequence_1  # noqa: F821
        if raw is not None:
            seq = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    except Exception:
        seq = None

    if seq is None or len(seq) < 128:
        seq = None

    if seq is not None:
        seq = np.where(np.isfinite(seq), seq, 0.0)
        seq = np.clip(seq, 0.0, None)
        if float(np.sum(seq)) <= 0.0 or len(seq) < 128:
            seq = None

    if seq is None:
        nbase = 8192
        seq = analytic_seed(nbase, gamma=1.5, phase=0.43, rng=rng)
    else:
        nbase = int(len(seq))
        if nbase > 32768:
            seq = seq[:32768]
            nbase = int(len(seq))

    # Work internally with density y, sum(y)=n.
    best_y = seq / float(np.sum(seq)) * float(nbase)
    best_y = project_simplex(best_y, float(nbase))
    best_y = orient_decreasing(best_y)
    best_obj = objective_y(best_y)

    print(f"Initial: len={len(best_y)}, internal_obj={best_obj:.7f}")

    def now_remaining():
        return max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())

    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 1: polish the provided construction at its original length.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    rem = now_remaining()
    t1 = min(350.0, 0.22 * rem)
    if t1 > 30.0:
        print(f"\nPhase 1: mirror-LSE at n={nbase}, budget={t1:.0f}s")
        y1, obj1 = mirror_optimize(
            best_y, t1, rng,
            mode="lse",
            score_start=8.0, score_end=35.0,
            eta_start=0.35, eta_end=1.0,
            momentum=0.85, patience_start=300,
            verbose=True
        )
        if obj1 < best_obj:
            best_y = y1
            best_obj = obj1
            print(f"  Phase 1 best obj={best_obj:.7f}, len={len(best_y)}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 2: alternate weighted local search / perturbation.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    rem = now_remaining()
    t2 = min(250.0, 0.12 * rem)
    if t2 > 30.0 and len(best_y) <= nbase:
        print(f"\nPhase 2: alternate weights at n={len(best_y)}, budget={t2:.0f}s")
        y_start = best_y + rng.normal(0.0, 0.04, len(best_y))
        y_start = project_simplex(y_start, float(len(y_start)))
        y2, obj2 = mirror_optimize(
            y_start, t2, rng,
            mode="soft",
            score_start=5.0, score_end=30.0,
            eta_start=0.45, eta_end=1.2,
            momentum=0.82, patience_start=220,
            q=2.0, verbose=False
        )
        if obj2 < best_obj:
            best_y = y2
            best_obj = obj2
            print(f"  Phase 2 best obj={best_obj:.7f}, len={len(best_y)}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 3: upsample to 2x and optimize.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    n2 = min(32768, 2 * len(best_y))
    rem = now_remaining()
    if n2 > len(best_y) and rem > 350.0:
        t3 = min(850.0, 0.45 * rem)
        print(f"\nPhase 3: upsample to n={n2}, budget={t3:.0f}s")
        y_up = upsample_y(best_y, n2)
        y_up = orient_decreasing(y_up)
        y3, obj3 = mirror_optimize(
            y_up, t3, rng,
            mode="lse",
            score_start=7.0, score_end=32.0,
            eta_start=0.28, eta_end=0.95,
            momentum=0.83, patience_start=350,
            verbose=True
        )
        if obj3 < best_obj:
            best_y = y3
            best_obj = obj3
            print(f"  Phase 3 best obj={best_obj:.7f}, len={len(best_y)}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 4: try one more coarser resolution toward 32768.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    n4 = min(32768, 2 * len(best_y))
    rem = now_remaining()
    if n4 > len(best_y) and rem > 220.0:
        t4 = min(320.0, 0.25 * rem)
        print(f"\nPhase 4: upsample to n={n4}, budget={t4:.0f}s")
        y_up = upsample_y(best_y, n4)
        y_up = orient_decreasing(y_up)
        y4, obj4 = mirror_optimize(
            y_up, t4, rng,
            mode="lse",
            score_start=9.0, score_end=38.0,
            eta_start=0.22, eta_end=0.85,
            momentum=0.80, patience_start=300,
            verbose=False
        )
        if obj4 < best_obj:
            best_y = y4
            best_obj = obj4
            print(f"  Phase 4 best obj={best_obj:.7f}, len={len(best_y)}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 5: final polish on the best currently found length.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    rem = now_remaining()
    if rem > 60.0:
        t5 = rem - 20.0
        print(f"\nPhase 5: final polish at n={len(best_y)}, budget={t5:.0f}s")
        y5, obj5 = mirror_optimize(
            best_y, t5, rng,
            mode="lse",
            score_start=12.0, score_end=55.0,
            eta_start=0.12, eta_end=0.55,
            momentum=0.72, patience_start=200,
            verbose=False
        )
        if obj5 < best_obj:
            best_y = y5
            best_obj = obj5
            print(f"  Phase 5 best obj={best_obj:.7f}, len={len(best_y)}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    # Return a scale-invariant, evaluator-friendly sequence.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    best_y = np.asarray(best_y, dtype=np.float64)
    best_y = np.clip(best_y, 0.0, None)
    ss = float(np.sum(best_y))
    if ss <= 0.0:
        best_y = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)
        ss = float(np.sum(best_y))

    final_a = best_y / ss
    final_a = np.clip(final_a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    final_list = [float(x) for x in final_a]

    # Optional sanity check against the official evaluator.
    eff = None
    try:
        eff = evaluate_sequence(final_list)  # noqa: F821
    except Exception:
        pass

    print("\n" + "=" * 60)
    print(f"FINAL: len={len(final_list)}")
    print(f"internal objective: {best_obj:.7f}")
    if eff is not None:
        print(f"evaluate_sequence: {eff:.7f}")
    print(f"total time: {time.monotonic() - total_start:.1f}s")
    print("=" * 60)

    if eff is not None and (not np.isfinite(eff)):
        fallback = analytic_seed(8192, gamma=1.5, phase=0.43, rng=rng)
        fallback = fallback / float(np.sum(fallback))
        return [float(x) for x in fallback]

    return final_list
```
